- **display 87.3**, p. 87, proof of Corollary 7.8: equivalent. All three lines are available for the constructed fields on the domain where the paper asserts them; they are spread over several declarations and are stated before the auxiliary-torus average and for all nine tensor components. Lines 1 and 2 are pure algebra with no NativeData hypothesis. covariance_increment_exact gives C(U+V) - C(U) = B(U,V) + B(V,U) + B(V,V) for the 3x3 angular-average tensor (the paper's B(U,V) + C(V) with B symmetrized, (7.23) on p. 82). incrementTensor_split (via covariance_increment_split) and signedRemainder_exact split it as symmetricCovariance primary tangent + symmetricCovariance (old - primary) tangent + symmetricCovariance old curl + bilinearCovariance (tangent+curl) (tangent+curl), i.e. B(W_0^as, L^as Sigma) + B(E, L^as Sigma) + B(U,R) + C(V) with E = U - W_0^as. Applying meanBar and taking components (0,1), (0,2) gives the paper's two-vector identity. Line 3 (B(W_0^as, L^as Sigma) = Sigma, the paper's (7.36)). In the paper this holds on the active domain 0 < q < q_big <= 2^{-l_0}, with labels restricted to bands l >= l_0 (pp. 62, 64, (6.8); p. 65 states the squared-partition identity "on the active shell"). The Lean mirrors this: labels are the cells of band >= N = (choice B N0).prepared.N; requested_cross_factor states, for every band n and every point of ActualInitialization.geometry.strip.domain, meanBar (actualCross 0 i.succ) n x = partitionFactor B N0 n x * requestedStress ... n x i; partitionFactor_eq_one gives partitionFactor = 1 whenever physicalScale n x <= Q N. So Sigma exactly holds wherever the physical scale is at most Q N, which contains the paper's domain when N plays the role of l_0. requested_cross_tail and family_requested_cross_tail package this for whole bands n >= N+1 (physicalScale_tail); the part of band N with coordinateQ <= 1 follows from the same two declarations. family_cross_eq transfers it to SignedMeanGain.crossTensor of the cycle's family. Outside that domain (bands n <= N-1 and the part of band N with coordinateQ > 1, all at physical scale > Q N) the Lean also carries charts the paper does not have; there requested_cross_defect gives cross - Sigma = -missingWeight * Sigma, and the main route (CorrectionAnalyticStep.step -> SignedCrossDefectClass.residual_defects_all_exponents_of_primitive -> CrossBasedMeanComposition.signed_mean_gain_of_cross_defects) writes meanBar(cross) = Sigma + crossDefect and keeps the defect, which vanishes from band N+1 on. This is additional to the display, not a gap in it. Remaining differences: Sigma in these statements is the requested stress of an arbitrary context and state (requestedStress c u), not an arbitrary two-vector in M_alpha; the chain is never assembled into one declaration; the identification of N with l_0 and of physicalScale with q is read off the label type and mask sums, and no Lean object for q_big was looked for. The earlier pairing's SignedMeanGain.native_family_cross (:1284) stays dropped: it takes SignedMeanGain.NativeData G, which no_legacy_nativeData shows is empty on ActualInitialization.geometry. SignedMeanGain.native_physical_cross (:510) has no NativeData hypothesis (it needs 1 <= N, 0 < q, q <= Q N and a cone condition) and states the cross identity for an arbitrary sigma on the model nativeAssembly; nothing links it to the constructed blocks, so "uses" at most. - **(9.14)**, p. 110, proof of Proposition 9.6: equivalent. For the constructed fields the display is stated by CrossBasedMeanComposition.bandSignedStage_mean_debt_of_cross_defects (CrossBasedMeanComposition.lean:271), which has no NativeData argument and holds for every SignedMeanGain.Geometry, including ActualInitialization.geometry. Its last five conjuncts are, for the stage output v = waveStage ... (the state after pressure reconstruction): the theta and axial residuals in MeanClass G.strip (1+sigma-2*kappa), their meanBar averages in MeanClass G.strip (1+sigma+17/100), and DefectBounds G.slowStrip (sigma-2*kappa) c v, which unfolds to UnweightedClass G.slowStrip (1+(sigma-2*kappa)) for each of the three debt rows (P, J_theta, J_z). With C_* = 1+sigma these are the four clauses of (9.14) with the same exponents, so the display follows by projecting conjuncts and unfolding DefectBounds; the classes are uniform in the band n, so the statement covers all bands with no band restriction in the conclusion. This theorem is the one on the path to the main chain: CorrectionAnalyticStep.step calls CycleParameters.mean_gain_from_waves_of_cross_defects, which calls fourStage_mean_gain_of_cross_defects, whose proof obtains the (9.14) block from bandSignedStage_mean_debt_of_cross_defects and hands it to CorrectionStep.meanStages_constructed; ActualStageEstimates.RunData.result applies step on ActualInitialization.geometry with kappa = ChartScales.kappa = 1/100000. What differs from the paper is the route to the hypothesis, not the display. The paper gets the bar gain from exact equality of the cross term with Sigma. The Lean theorem instead assumes the literal cross defect meanBar(crossTensor) - physicalSigma lies in MeanClass (1+sigma+17/100+kappa); on the actual chart the exact identity is only available for bands n >= (choice B N0).prepared.N + 1 (ActualSignedMeanBinding.family_requested_cross_tail, used as StepData.cross_tail by ActualCyclePreservation.stepData_of_waves), and SignedCrossDefectClass.residual_defects_all_exponents_of_primitive promotes that tail identity plus a baseline class to every exponent, keeping the finitely many earlier bands in the constant. The full four-clause conjunction is not exported as a single declaration specialised to the actual iterates: for them only E_theta, E_z in M_H (StepResult.afterSignedTheta/afterSignedAxial) and (P, J_theta, J_z) in S_H (ActualIntermediateDebtBounds.stage_debt_from_stepData, on ActualInitialization.slowStrip) are stated separately, while the averaged clause at C_*+0.17 is stated only in the general theorems at lines 140 and 271 and consumed inside the proof of fourStage_mean_gain_of_cross_defects. The earlier pairing cited SignedMeanGain.native_signed_mean_gain as stating the mean clauses; that theorem takes B : SignedMeanGain.NativeData G, which ActualSignedMeanBinding.no_legacy_nativeData shows is empty on ActualInitialization.geometry, so it is vacuous on the actual chart and is dropped. The NativeData chain (native_signed_mean_gain, BandReindexedSignedMeanGain.native_signed_mean_gain, CorrectionStep nativeSignedStage_mean_debt and bandNativeSignedStage_mean_debt, CorrectionStep fourStage_mean_gain, CycleParameters.mean_gain_from_waves) all take SignedMeanGain.NativeData G and ends at mean_gain_from_waves, which has no caller. GaugeDebtIncrement.waveStage_defectBounds and the four definitions are unaffected and kept; waveStage_defectBounds is instantiated with alpha = 1+sigma-kappa and tau = sigma-2*kappa, not at C_*+0.17. ## Structural notes

From the repository atlas (`work/atlas-result.json`, `arch.notes`):

The "table" on pp. 14-15 is the pair of flowcharts Figures 5 (p13) and 6 (p15).

Structural findings:
1. The paper's intermediate results have no separate Lean statements. No declaration states Theorem 3.1, Proposition 9.9, Proposition 10.1 or Lemmas 10.2/10.3 on their own. They are combined inside GermCandidateAssembly.exists_candidate_witness_of_finite_stages (GermCandidateAssembly.lean:164), whose output is a periodic candidate (ProblemStatement.CandidateProperties, ProblemStatement.lean:101). Theorem 3.1(iii) is restated as a joint-jet limit at (1,0) (VanishingJointJets). Theorem 3.1(ii) is restated as AwayExtensions.
2. The R3 route goes through a periodic step. The paper builds the R3 fields and force directly (Prop 10.1, Lemma 10.3). Lean periodizes and time-activates the cut sums, builds a Borel force for the periodized residual, and then cuts back to R3. The cut-back uses R3CompactCandidate.of_localized_fields, an outer force cutoff (compactForce) and a time cutoff PositiveTimeForce.force; neither cutoff appears in the paper. Corollary 10.6 then periodizes the R3 candidate again, which is the paper's route. A native periodic adapter (option_D_of_candidate) exists but is unused.
3. The blowup is shown differently. Lean uses the axial velocity at the origin, u(t,0) = (1-t)^{-A} j e_z (FinalSlowBase.origin, FinalSlowBase.lean:361). The paper uses the swirl path of Theorem 3.1(iv)/(3.6). Nothing on the main path states (3.6).
4. Lemma 10.4 on the main path is a weaker Gronwall bound. The sharp F(t)^2 inequality is proved (candidate_energy_estimates) but feeds only theorem_1_1_with_dissipation. The maximal-lifespan and H3 claims of p124 exist only for the periodic step (CandidateConsequences.Consequences) and are unused.
5. Steps that exist only in Lean: the comparator adapters, the initial-rest clause |t| <= 3/8 (needed for the compression), and the fixed support cylinder (radius 1/4, height 1/2) in place of the paper's q*-dependent cutoff.
6. The decay exponent sigma_j = 1/5 + j/10 appears only as an abstract gain sequence tending to infinity (StageEstimates.gain).
7. Docstrings cite a different numbering ("Proposition 11.4", "Lemma 11.3", "Proposition 11.7", "Lemma 3.3", "Proposition 5.1"), which does not match this manuscript. Treat docstring cross-references as unreliable.
8. Profile and wave mappings (Section 3.2, Section 3.3) rest only on module docs and were not checked item by item.

Mechanical checks (a comment- and string-aware scan of all 2659 `.lean` files at the pinned commit):

- `NavierStokes/`: 0 `sorry`, 0 `admit`, 0 `axiom` declarations, 0 `native_decide`.
- `sorry` anywhere: 4 occurrences, all in `ComparatorChallenges/Euler.lean`, `ComparatorChallenges/NavierStokes.lean` (Comparator's challenge placeholders).
- Comparator's permitted axioms (`ComparatorChallenges/NavierStokes.json`): `propext`, `Quot.sound`, `Classical.choice`.
